hits Amenadiel in the face and Amenadiel insists that it doesn’t count. Unimpressed, Lucifer
beats him well aware that Amenadiel has to throw the fight. He continues taunting Amenadiel
until Amendiel fights back. Lucifer says that Amenadiel is just as prideful as he is and the two
of them fight for real. Amenadiel finally gets control of himself and says that he won’t stoop to
Lucifer’s level because he’s better than he is. When he turns away, Lucifer grabs him in a choke
hold and tells him to fight. Amenadiel surrenders and Lucifer is declared the winner.
    The next day, Chloe and Dan run surveillance on Tio. Tio told Charlotte that he’d help them
find Aiden’s killer, and he’s going to drop the money off at a locker. Dan points out that if Tio
doesn’t follow the plan, they’ve lost. He worries that it will set her back, and Chloe says that the
job is about taking risks.
    As Tio goes to the lockers they’re watching, Amenadiel and Lucifer are watching nearby.
Amenadiel says that they’ve both stayed there far too long. Gil shows up and goes to the locker,
and Chloe sends Dan to arrest Tio while she confronts Gil. Gil runs around the corner and Chloe
hears him scream. By the time she gets there, the bag is on the ground but there’s no sign of Gil.
    Lucifer and Amenadiel take Gil to a safe location and ask him where the necklace is. Gil
remembers that he shot Amenadiel, and offers to replace the necklace. Amenadiel insists that
God gave it to him, and Gil says that Aiden was like a son to him but refused to cooperate when
Gil ran into money trouble. They fought and Gil shot him and tried to make it look like a robbery.
Amenadiel demands his necklace, and Gil says that it’s in his safe at the gym. Lucifer is intrigued
that Gil killed his ”son” for refusing to play in his part, and reveals his devil face to Gil.
    Soon, Chloe and Dan find Gil on the beach. He confesses that he killed Aiden and Dan con-
gratulates Chloe.
    That night, Amenadiel finds Lucifer at Rico’s bar that he’s bought. Lucifer says that he’s not
leaving, because he’s realized that he’s no longer an angel. If that’s true then there’s no reason for
him to please God. Lucifer is abandoning God because God abandoned him, and he’s accepting
his role as the rebel. Amenadiel insists that Lucifer come back with him to Hell, and Lucifer calls
in his favor from earlier. He tells Amenadiel to leave him alone, and reminds Amenadiel that it’s a
sin for an angel to break a vow. Amenadiel warns that Lucifer will suffer God’s wrath, and Lucifer
tells him that God knows where to find him.
    Lucifer goes to the beach and Maze joins him. He extends his wings and following his orders,
Maze cuts them off with her blade.
    Smiling, Lucifer looks up at Heaven.

attacked her. There was some hair under her nails so Ella needs to examine it. Ella says that the
person who called the police was Beckett, her next patient. Beckett might have tried to wipe the
blood away from the wound.
   Dan is taking Beckett to the station. He knows she’s traumatized, but he asks about wiping
the blood away. She doesn’t think she did, but she can’t really remember. She wants a Slurpee.
Dan is happy to oblige.
   Ella is about to leave when she spots some blood on a doorknob. She opens the door and
finds a little boy inside.
   Chloe speaks with the boy’s father. Felix is not a suspect, but he might have been there right
after Beth was killed. His father says that Felix is shy, and he might not be able to communicate.
Beth was able to help him a lot, and he’ll be lost without her.
   Meanwhile, Ella sits with Felix and helps him get the blood off his hands. She tells him about
being in a car accident when she was a kid. It was scary, but he’ll be okay. Felix tells her that he
didn’t see who did it. His mom arrives, and the parents leave with their son.
   At the station, Ella studies the evidence when her friend Ray-Ray shows up. Ella can’t believe
she’s there, because they made a pact not to see each other since Detroit. Chloe walks in, and

                                                 223
                                       Lucifer Episode Guide


we see that Ella is there all by herself. Talking to herself. Chloe asks if she’s okay, but Ella says
she’s talking to herself. She tries to make it like she was talking to the evidence. The hair was
plush fur used in ketchup. While she talks, Ray is right there with her.
    When Chloe leaves, Ray tells Ella she knows she is trying to avoid her. As it happens, she can
see ghosts. Ella tells the ghost that she likes who she is now, and it’s better to not have secrets.
Ella spent her childhood avoiding the ghosts because she was labeled as being crazy. Ray says
she’s there to help with work stuff. Ray says she knows the murder victim, so she’s there to help
her.
    Dan tells Lucifer that he lost Beckett, who also took his phone. Lucifer chides him, and Dan
came to Lucifer for help. He’ll help Dan, but he’s also going to make fun of him.
    Ella and Chloe arrive at the apartment, and Ella tells her ghost to shush, which makes Chloe
suspicious. When Chloe goes upstairs, Ella tries to give her ghost ground rules. No talking. Ella
finds some costumes, and it turns out she used to cosplay to avoid the ghosts. Chloe comes
in and sees her with the costume, and Ella explains that furries get a bad wrap. They find the
costume has the same ketchup stain. There’s a message on the answering machine, leading them
to the next scene.
    Chloe dons the costume and goes to a furry convention to find out who the mysterious Wesley.
Chloe ends up talking dolphin with a dolphin, so Ella asks Ray-Ray for help, to no avail. It turns
out she doesn’t know the murder victim. She’s really there because there is some unfinished
business. Ella thinks solving the case will help her cross over.
    Chloe is still looking for Wesley when another furry attacks her. A full fur fight ensues. Ella
rushes to help Chloe as a crowd of onlookers watches. Chloe tears off her mask and holds up
her badge.
    As it happens, ”Wesley” is the attacker’s original character and Beth stole it. She was so upset
she threw ketchup on her. She thinks Beth was addicted to conflict, and she was always fighting.
She has an alibi, though.
    We travel to Magic Mountain, where Lucifer and Dan track Dan’s phone. Dan hates amuse-
ment parks. He loved roller coasters until he was 12 and he took a girl on one after a bunch of
chili dogs. Beckett is on Viper. Dan gets on the ride with her, but she jumps off. Dan gets buckled
in and Lucifer catches Beckett while Dan goes for a ride.
    Ella returns to the crime scene and explains to Ray-Ray that she likes to check out the crime
scene. She wants to go to the beach, but Ella is only concerned with helping her to cross over.
She’s lying about it. She admits to checking up with Ella, and she knows she’s thinking about
moving home. Going back is a horrible idea, the ghost says, because her family uses her. The
people in LA don’t know about her ghost abilities but her family does. As they talk, someone
breaks in, so Ella hides in a closet. He goes to the desk and looks around, but then he hears Ella
and opens the closet door. She shines a light in his eyes and runs out, and then they fight. He
runs away, but she has his DNA on the broom.
    Lucifer checks in with Dan after the ride. He bought a shirt with Dan’s picture on it. In the
process, they lost Beckett again. She also got away with Dan’s badge and Lucifer’s keys. She
sends them a text: ”Sorry, not sorry, losers.”
    Chloe uses traffic cameras to track down the car that left after Ella was attacked. There was
chalk on the broom, and the car belongs to a teacher. Felix left his violin in Beth’s office, so they
go to talk to the music teacher. Later, when they interview him, he admits to being in her office
and he says he was getting a check he wrote to stop her from sending Felix to a home school
problem, taking him away from him. When they mention Beckett the music teacher says that
she’s a ”nightmare.”
    Chloe calls Dan to find out where they are. He’s out chasing Lucifer’s car, and now that Chloe
says Beckett is a suspect, they need to bring her in. Lucifer makes a call to the person best suited
to track down humans: Maze. Maze arrived in a sweet Audi convertible and she wants to know
what the ”idiots” have done now.
    Maze laughs when she hears how they lost a 14-year-old girl who took their car and badge.
She’s game to find Beckett just to meet her. It doesn’t take her long to find Beckett at Lux, who
is there using Dan’s badge to get drinks. Dan tells her that she’s a suspect, and she freaks out.
He wants to know the truth, and she insists that she wasn’t there. She put on an act to get out
of class. Dan also says that Beckett is allergic to perfume, and the office smelled like perfume
when she arrived.

                                                224
                                       Lucifer Episode Guide


    Ella is back in the lab when Chloe comes in. She wants to know what’s going on, and Ella
makes up an answer. But Chloe sees through it and wants to know if she needs to talk about
moving back home. She understands needing her family, but the station is also her family. Chloe
has an idea about suspects.
    They head to the next scene and Ella hangs back to talk to Ray Ray, who understands why
Ella didn’t tell Chloe the truth. Ray-Ray says her friends are real, but Ella says Chloe likes to see
the evidence. As they talk, she spots a construction dumpster.
    Chloe talks to Felix’s mother, who wears freesia perfume. She praises her son’s violin talents,
and Chloe asks about Felix and home school. But Chloe has the wrong story — it was Felix’s
father who wanted him to do home school.
    Ella digs through the trash and finds the murder weapon, complete with blood. But then
Felix’s father arrives and attempts to hit her with a bat. They face off, with Ray-Ray watching.
Chloe runs up and stops Anthony from killing her. Felix was at the crime scene because he
followed his father there. Anthony killed Beth because she was making him worse, but Ella
argues that she only helped him see how special he it.
    Later, Ella blurts out that she sees ghosts to Chloe, who wants to hear the story. Ella says she
was in a bad wreck and that’s when she saw Ray-Ray, but she kept coming back over the years.
When she grew up she realized she wasn’t imaginary, and eventually the ghost revealed that she
was a ghost. Ella was worried that Chloe would think she’s crazy, but Chloe is very supportive
and doesn’t think she’s crazy. She admits to seeing crazier things, and she says nothing would
ever change how she feels about Ella. They hug, and Ella says she isn’t going to move back to
Detroit. They high-five.
    Ray-Ray tells Ella that her friends are great. Now that the case is closed, she’s ready to go.
Ella asks if she has been there to watch over her since the accident, and Ray-Ray says she has
been there to watch her back. Ella says she can stick around, but not when she’s in the shower.
    Lucifer unveils a banner of Dan on the roller coaster. Ella tells Lucifer she will ”smell you
later,” and he asks where she heard that. Later, Lucifer goes home and calls out Azrael, a.k.a.
Ray-Ray. She apologizes for not showing up sooner, but the whole family was rocked when God
kicked Lucifer out. She misses her favorite big brother, but he started a rebellion in Hell. He
wants to know how Azrael knows Ella. She admits that during that wreck she was supposed to
take Ella, but she couldn’t. So she kept watch over her, but she told Ella she was a ghost and
she sent Ella to LA to be there with Lucifer. Azrael did it for Ella, because she needed someone
and she knew Lucifer needed someone, so this way two of her favorite people could be together.
She disappears.
    Ella is back at work, and Lucifer shows up and smiles at her. He heard she isn’t leaving, and
he’s happy about it. He’s there to listen to her, and she excitedly tells him all about her new
project. Azrael watches over them both, smiling.

                               Once Upon a Time
                                         Season 3
                                    Episode Number: 57
                                    Season Episode: 26

   Originally aired:   Monday May 28, 2018
   Writer:             Ricardo Lopez Jr., Ildy Modrovich, Joe Henderson
   Director:           Kevin Alejandro
   Show Stars:         Tom Ellis (Lucifer Morningstar), Lauren German (Chloe Decker), Kevin
                       Alejandro (Dan Espinoza), Lesley-Ann Brandt (Mazikeen), Aimee Gar-
                       cia (Ella Lopez), Tom Welling (Marcus Pierce), Rachael Harris (Linda
                       Martin)
   Guest Stars:        Tricia Helfer (Charlotte), D.B. Woodside (Amenadiel), Scarlett Estevez
                       (Trixie), Louis Herthum (Lt. John Decker), Brian Tee (Ben Rogers),
                       David Meunier (Rex Wilson), Neil Gaiman (God), Al Coronel (Nick
                       Seals), Selkie Hom (Erika Dunlap), Jermaine Love (Nathan Fury),
                       Rocky Myers (Stryder Novac), Will Roberts (Crew Chief), Jon Ryckman
                       (Lead Guard), Rachele Schank (Charlynn), John Tague (Grizzled Cop),
                       Nikea Gamby-Turner (Stacy Zallis)
   Production Code:    T13.20772
   Summary:            Set in an alternate universe created by God in which Lucifer and Chloe
                       never meet.


                                                       The episode begins with Chloe on the run
                                                       as she is chasing a suspect. Men jump
                                                       out of a van with guns raised and they
                                                       shoot her. She falls to the ground, slowly.
                                                          A voice tells us that there was a world
                                                       with a small change that changed every-
                                                       thing.
                                                          Chloe is about to die, and her old part-
                                                       ner (who is actually the same partner who
                                                       set her up and tried to frame Dan in sea-
                                                       son 1) runs up. She’s about to die when a
                                                       voice calls ”cut” and the director praises
                                                       her. The stunt director talk about stunts
                                                       and he reveals that he might not be there
                                                       for the final stunt. He asks her out for a
drink.
   Later, Lucifer plays for a crowd at Lux. When he finishes, Lucifer is there and he watches
Chloe come in. Charlotte Richards is also there (she’s alive!), and she is his lawyer. She has the
paperwork for his Vegas expansion.
   The voice says that the ”butterfly effect” can bring people back from the dead.
   Upstairs, Lucifer can’t sleep with her. Something is wrong. He thinks he might have been in
LA too long, which is why he wants to move to Las Vegas. He opens his safe and reveals tons of
cash to use in Las Vegas.
   Dr. Linda meets with Chloe, who says she’s frustrated with her life. She talks about work
and the car chase, but she’s bored. Linda points out that she has played this role five times
before, and she originally wanted to be a role model for women. Chloe remarks that they really
reinvented Linda’s office, and the camera pulls back to reveal that the office is a set.
   The voice says that things would have been very different if he hadn’t intervened and brought
Lucifer and Chloe together. (As he talks, Lucifer goes downstairs and finds a dead body there.)
The voice says that he can show what things would be like because he’s God.

                                               227
                                       Lucifer Episode Guide


    Dan shows up to meet with Vick, his partner. Dan goes to talk with Lucifer, who calls him
Detective Dildo. Lucifer challenges him because this wasn’t a bar fight, and he’s upset that his
Vegas expansion has been blocked because of the murder.
    God says that he has a complicated relationship with his son. He reveals that things were
different for Chloe because in this life, he moved a bullet slightly and her father is alive. Chloe
shows up to see her dad and find out about her friend, the stunt director who died at Lux. She
doesn’t think he’d do anything to screw up his dream job. She wants to talk to Dan, but he says
this isn’t a movie set.
    Lucifer shows up in a red convertible to see Ella, a tattooed version of herself. With a big gun.
He’s there to find out about a more ”delicate” matter. The gun isn’t loaded, it’s just for look. He
has a tool with him that was used in the murder, and she tells him about the tool and says
it could have come from a NASCAR tool kit. Crews have coded tools. He’s going to look at the
nearest racetrack, and he pays her before leaving.
    When Lucifer shows up, the track security think he’s LAPD, but Chloe is there, too, pretending
to be a cop. Lucifer walks up to her and smiles. ”Hello, detective,” he says. He knows she’s an
actress, not an officer. He’s a fan of her work and wants to know why she’s there. She admits
she worked with Strider and doesn’t believe that he died in a bar fight. He introduces himself,
and she knows he owns Lux, but he points out that he has a VIP pass and she has a badge that
belongs to Warner Bros. As they talk, Chloe is given a pass that gives her even more access than
he has. ”Game on,” Lucifer says.
    Dan is taking money from evidence when Charlotte finds him. She wants to know if he’s tired
skimming money, and she’s tired of getting him out of trouble. She tells him about the money in
the safe at Lux, and if he can get her in, she can steal the money. He agrees to help so long as
she doesn’t get caught.
    Chloe looks for Rex, but he’s already on his practice run. Lucifer walks out onto the track and
stands in front of the car. He wills it to stop, but it’s Amenadiel who stops the car and freezes
time. Amenadiel is there and Lucifer says he has been responding a lot faster lately. It turns out
Amenadiel can’t return because he is afraid of being judged by their family.
    Time returns, and Rex has been pulled from the car. Track security stops Lucifer while Chloe
walks away with Rex. She gives Lucifer a smile and a wave as she walks away.
    Chloe demands answers from Rex. She has no problem using violence to get answers, includ-
ing a shot to Rex’s crotch. Lucifer walks in and blows Chloe’s cover, but Rex can’t hide from
Lucifer’s charms. Rex wants a good story, like Strider’s father. People don’t follow cars anymore,
and he wanted to create a good story. He opened a management company and offered Strider a
job.
    Outside, Chloe asks Lucifer how he got him to talk. He says he isn’t a Jedi, but people like to
tell him things. She says they’re after the same thing, so maybe they should work together. He
isn’t interested, but Chloe knows who Strider was fighting with.
    Linda sees a client for her show. She’s like Dr. Phil with boobs. Her assistant gives her things
to talk about during their session, but she’s not willing to make her client upset for ratings. She’s
torn.
    Chloe brings Lucifer to the set, and they see Erica Dunlap, Chloe’s costar. The beef was that
Strider wouldn’t testify on her behalf when she got hurt on set, and she’s hoping Lucifer will go
to her trailer and work his magic.
    When Chloe’s dad shows up on set he knows she might have been playing cop, and he hopes
she hasn’t been hanging out with Lucifer. Suddenly the air is filled with Lucifer’s name. He’s
sleeping with the actress... .
    Charlotte shows up to Lucifer’s penthouse and opens the safe. She thinks she’s alone, but as
she grabs the money, it’s clear that she is not alone at all. Maze is there with half of her face
covered up. Maze wants to know why she wants the money, and it’s not until Charlotte tells the
truth that she is satisfied with the answer. Maze frees her, encouraging her to go out and be bad,
and she promises that they’ll meet again ”somewhere hotter.”
    Chloe’s dad is upset that she impersonated a cop. Lucifer emerges from the trailer covered in
lipstick. She hoped Lucifer would get the information, but instead her father sends her home.
Lucifer tells her that he’s an expert in upsetting fathers. He also reveals that Erica can wrap her
legs around his neck, so she was faking her injuries. They’re still back in the game.

                                                228
                                     Lucifer Episode Guide


    They return to Ella’s garage, and she’s upset that Lucifer brought someone, but when she
sees Chloe she freaks out because Chloe was a huge inspiration for her. Ella reveals that ”Last
Lappers” was written on the tool, and Rodgers, who was also on the stunt team, was in a movie
called Last Lappers. He could have been working with Strider.
    They arrive on set and hope to find Ben Rogers there. They’re thinking that Rogers might have
been looking for something to steal ... like the $6 million car that Chloe was supposed to drive.
Chloe gets into her car and tells Lucifer she trained for this moment. She blocks him in and they
confront Rogers, who denies being the killer. When she mentions prints on the murder weapon,
he runs off. They chase him, and Lucifer catches him. Rogers says Strider backed out of the deal,
and he shoots Lucifer. The Devil Face comes out but Chloe shows up and knocks him out before
she can see it.
    Lucifer and Chloe are talking when her father shows up. Lucifer says that he underestimated
his daughter, and Lt. Decker says she was brave. She wants to know why he pushed her away
from being a cop, and he admits that it scared him. He says a parent just wants what’s best for
his child. They hug, and Lucifer watches thoughtfully. Lucifer thinks their fathers might not be
so dissimilar.
    God says that choice is a funny thing. We see Ella with a signed poster, and Maze roasting
a new mask for a new inductee in her cult, Linda as she makes her client cry, Amenadiel as he
goes to Lux and wants to meet someone but hesitates. Dan and Charlotte drive away, happily
ever after.
    Chloe and Lucifer share a drink in the lights of Griffith Park. She says the experience of
working with him was fulfilling. She might be tired of being an actor, and maybe she wants to be
a real cop. Lucifer promises to tag along.
    God says that sometimes people are drawn together anyway. But in the end, isn’t he better
off for bringing them together?
